\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt\leq\Upsilon_{2}(k_{0})\left(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}+\frac{\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\beta_{k}^{2}}\left\{\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\beta_{k}}\right)^{2q}\right\}\right)\quad\text{ (Lemma \ref{technical lemma:2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt\leq\Upsilon_{3}(k_{0})\left(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}+\left(\alpha_{k}/\beta_{k}\right)^{2(q+1)}\right).

(C.15)

In addition, by Lemma
C.1
, we also have for some constant
\Upsilon_{4}>0
such that for any
k\geq k_{0}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{k+1}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{1-2(1-\epsilon){\bar{\alpha}}_{k}\right\}\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k+1}\right]+\Upsilon_{4}\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{1-\frac{2(1-\epsilon)\nu_{k}\alpha_{k}}{\tau_{k}\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c}}\right\}\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k+1}\right]+\Upsilon_{4}\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left\{1-2(1-\epsilon)\zeta\alpha_{k}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\right]+\Upsilon_{4}\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\right],

where the last inequality uses the fact that
2(1-\epsilon)\zeta\alpha_{k}<1
(it holds for
k
large enough with deterministic threshold index). Applying the above inequality recursively with the bound in (
C.5
), we know for some constant
\Upsilon_{5}(k_{0})>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{k+1}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{5}(k_{0})\sum_{i=k_{0}}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}\left\{1-2\zeta(1-\epsilon)\alpha_{j}\right\}\alpha_{i}\left(\beta_{i}+b_{i}^{4}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\beta_{i}}\right)^{2(q+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{5}(k_{0})\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}\left\{1-2\zeta(1-\epsilon)\alpha_{j}\right\}\alpha_{i}\left(\beta_{i}+b_{i}^{4}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\beta_{i}}\right)^{2(q+1)}\right).

By Lemma
A.2
and the condition
2\zeta\iota_{1}(1-\epsilon)>1
when
p_{1}=1
, we know

\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}\left\{1-2\zeta(1-\epsilon)\alpha_{j}\right\}\alpha_{i}\beta_{i}=O(\beta_{k}).

Without loss of generality, we suppose
\beta_{k}=o(b_{k}^{4}+(\alpha_{k}/\beta_{k})^{2(q+1)})
; otherwise the result is trivial. Then, Lemma
A.2
also leads to

\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}\left\{1-2\zeta(1-\epsilon)\alpha_{j}\right\}\alpha_{i}\left(b_{i}^{4}+\left(\alpha_{i}/\beta_{i}\right)^{2(q+1)}\right)=O\left(b_{k}^{4}+\left(\alpha_{k}/\beta_{k}\right)^{2(q+1)}\right).

Combining the above three displays, we obtain

\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{z}}_{k+1}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k+1}\right]\leq\Upsilon_{6}(k_{0})\left(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}+\left(\alpha_{k}/\beta_{k}\right)^{2(q+1)}\right).

(C.16)

Combining (
C.5
) and (
C.16
), we prove that the result holds for
q+1
. This completes the induction step and concludes the proof.

C.6
Proof of Lemma
4.7

For notational conciseness, we follow Appendix
C.3
and use
\Upsilon_{1},\Upsilon_{2},\ldots
to denote generic deterministic constants. We note that

\|{\widetilde{W}}_{k}-W^{\star}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{def:acc2},\eqref{equ:def:tau}}}{{=}}\left\|\begin{pmatrix}{\bar{B}}_{k}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}^{\star}&({\bar{G}}_{k}-G^{\star})^{T}\\
{\bar{G}}_{k}-G^{\star}&{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\\
\leq 2\|{\bar{B}}_{k}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}^{\star}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}+2\|{\bar{G}}_{k}-G^{\star}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}.

(C.17)

We bound
\|{\bar{B}}_{k}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}^{\star}\|^{2}
as an example, while the bound of
\|{\bar{G}}_{k}-G^{\star}\|^{2}
can be derived in the same way with only fewer terms, resulting in the same upper bound. We have

\displaystyle{\bar{B}}_{k}

\displaystyle=(1-\beta_{k}){\bar{B}}_{k-1}+\beta_{k}{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{k}=(1-\beta_{k}){\bar{B}}_{k-1}+\beta_{k}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{k};\xi_{k})+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\lambda}}_{k}^{j}{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}c^{j}_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})+\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\lambda}}_{h}^{j}{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}c^{j}_{h}\right)+\prod_{h=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{h}){\bar{B}}_{-1}.

With the above expression, we have a similar decomposition to (
C.2
) and obtain

\displaystyle{\bar{B}}_{k}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}^{2}\mathcal{L}^{\star}=\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left(\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-\nabla^{2}f_{h}\right)+\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(\nabla^{2}f_{h}-\nabla^{2}f^{\star})

\displaystyle+\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\bm{\lambda}}_{h}^{j}-({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{j}\right){\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}c^{j}_{h}+\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\sum_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{j}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}c^{j}_{h}-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}c^{j}_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\sum_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{j}\left(\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}c^{j}_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-\nabla^{2}c^{j}_{h}\right)+\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\sum_{j=1}^{m}({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{j}\left(\nabla^{2}c^{j}_{h}-(\nabla^{2}c^{j})^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\prod_{h=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{h})({\bar{B}}_{-1}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}^{2}\mathcal{L}^{\star})\eqqcolon\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{2}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{3}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{4}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{5}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{6}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{7}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{8}^{k}.

We establish the bounds for
\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{2}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{3}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{4}^{k}
, while the bounds of
\mathcal{K}_{5}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{6}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{7}^{k}
can be derived similarly to those of
\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{2}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{3}^{k}
, and
\|\mathcal{K}_{8}^{k}\|^{2}=O(\prod_{h=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{h})^{2})\leq\exp(-2\sum_{h=0}^{k}\beta_{h})=o(\beta_{k})
by (
C.4
) only contributes to the higher-order error.

\bullet
For
\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}
, we know from the proof of Lemma
4.5
in Appendix
C.2
that there exist
0<\delta^{\prime}<\delta
, a deterministic threshold
K^{\prime}>0
, and a constant
\Upsilon_{1}>0
such that for any
k\geq K^{\prime}
and any
{\bm{x}}_{k}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}\coloneqq\mathcal{X}\cap\{{\bm{x}}:\|{\bm{x}}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}\|\leq\delta^{\prime}\}
, we have
\mathbb{E}[\|{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{k};\xi_{k})\|^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq\Upsilon_{1}
. With this property, we separate
\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}
into three terms:

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right)\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\notin\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{h=0}^{K^{\prime}-1}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right)\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{h=K^{\prime}}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right)\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}\eqqcolon\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{1,2}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{1,3}^{k}.

For
\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}
, since
{\bm{x}}_{h}\in\mathcal{X}
and
{\bm{x}}_{h}\rightarrow{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}
as
h\rightarrow\infty
almost surely (cf. Assumption
4.1
), we know for any run of the sequence
\{{\bm{x}}_{h}\}
, there exist a (potentially random)
\widetilde{h}<\infty
and a constant
\Upsilon_{2}(\widetilde{h})>0
such that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{\widetilde{h}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right)\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\notin\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=0}^{\widetilde{h}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left\|{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right\|\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\notin\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{\widetilde{h}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{\widetilde{h}}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\left\|{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\right\|\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\notin\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}\prod_{l=\widetilde{h}+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{nequ:7}}}}{{\leq}}\;\Upsilon_{2}(\widetilde{h})\exp\left(-\frac{\iota_{2}k^{1-p_{2}}}{1-p_{2}}\right).

This implies that

P\left(\bigcap_{M=0}^{\infty}\bigcap_{K=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{M,K}\right)\coloneqq P\left(\bigcap_{M=0}^{\infty}\bigcap_{K=0}^{\infty}\bigcup_{k\geq K}\left\{k\|\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}\|\geq M\right\}\right)=0.

Since
\mathcal{A}_{M+1,K+1}\subseteq\mathcal{A}_{M,K}
, we have
\lim_{M\rightarrow\infty,K\rightarrow\infty}P(\mathcal{A}_{M,K})=0
. Thus, for any
\epsilon>0
there exist
M(\epsilon)
and
K(\epsilon)
such that for any
k\geq K(\epsilon)
,
P(k\|\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}\|\geq M(\epsilon))\leq\epsilon
. This means that
\|\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}\|=O_{p}(1/k)
. Following the same analysis, we also obtain
\|\mathcal{K}_{1,2}^{k}\|=O_{p}(1/k)
. For
\mathcal{K}_{1,3}^{k}
, we apply the martingale difference property (noting that
\bm{1}_{{\bm{x}}_{h}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta^{\prime}}}
is
\mathcal{F}_{h-1}
-measurable) and the bounded second moment condition, and obtain

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{K}_{1,3}^{k}\|^{2}]\leq O\left(\sum_{h=K^{\prime}}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})^{2}\beta_{h}^{2}\right)\leq O\left(\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})^{2}\beta_{h}^{2}\right)=O(\beta_{k}),

where the last equality is due to Lemma
A.2
. Combining the results of
\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{1,2}^{k}
,
\mathcal{K}_{1,3}^{k}
, we have

\|\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\leq\|\mathcal{K}_{1}^{k}\|^{2}=O_{p}(\beta_{k}).

(C.18)

\bullet
For
\mathcal{K}_{2}^{k}
, we use
p_{4}>0.5p_{3}
, apply Lemmas
3.5
and
A.2
, and have

\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{K}_{2}^{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}]\leq O\left(\left\{\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(b_{h}+\widetilde{b}_{h}^{2}/b_{h})\right\}^{2}\right)=O(b_{k}^{2}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{4}/b_{k}^{2}).

(C.19)

\bullet
For
\mathcal{K}_{3}^{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{3}^{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\leq\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\|\nabla^{2}f_{h}-\nabla^{2}f^{\star}\|\cdot\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=0}^{k_{0}-1}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\|\nabla^{2}f_{h}-\nabla^{2}f^{\star}\|+\sum_{h=k_{0}}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\|\nabla^{2}f_{h}-\nabla^{2}f^{\star}\|\cdot\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>h}\eqqcolon\mathcal{K}_{3,1}^{k}+\mathcal{K}_{3,2}^{k}.

By the same analysis as in
\mathcal{K}_{1,1}^{k}
, we know
\mathcal{K}_{3,1}^{k}=O_{p}(1/k)
. For
\mathcal{J}_{3,2}^{k}
, we apply the Lipschitz continuity condition and Lemmas
4.6
and
A.2
, and have for some constants
\Upsilon_{3}>0,\Upsilon_{4}(k_{0})>0
,
\Upsilon_{5}(k_{0})>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\mathcal{K}_{3,2}^{k})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{3}\left(\sum_{h=k_{0}}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\{\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{x}}_{h}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>h}]\}^{1/2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{4}(k_{0})\left(\sum_{h=k_{0}}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(\sqrt{\beta_{h}}+b_{h}^{2})\right)^{2}\leq\Upsilon_{5}(k_{0})(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}).

Combining the above two displays, we have

\|\mathcal{K}_{3}^{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}=O_{p}(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}).

(C.20)

\bullet
For
\mathcal{K}_{4}^{k}
, we apply (
C.2
) and follow the same analysis as
\mathcal{J}_{3}^{k}
. We obtain for some constant
\Upsilon_{6}>0
that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{4}^{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{6}\left\{\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{h}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Upsilon_{6}\left\{\left(\sum_{h=0}^{k_{0}-1}+\sum_{h=k_{0}}^{k}\right)\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{h}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>h}\right\}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}\right).

(C.21)

Combining (
C.18
), (
C.19
), (
C.20
), (
C.6
), ignoring higher-order error terms, and establishing the same bounds for
\mathcal{J}_{5}^{k}
,
\mathcal{J}_{6}^{k}
,
\mathcal{J}_{7}^{k}
, we obtain

\|{\bar{B}}_{k}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}^{\star}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}=O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{2}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{4}/b_{k}^{2}\right).

Following the same analysis, we can derive the same bound for
\|{\bar{G}}_{k}-G^{\star}\|^{2}
. Plugging into (
C.17
), we complete the proof.

C.7
Proof of Theorem
4.8

To streamline the proof, we first present a generic lemma, which is proved in Appendix
C.8
. We will apply this lemma to various terms that appear throughout the proof.

Lemma C.3
.

Consider a sequence of random variables
\{X_{k}\}_{k=0}^{\infty}
and a sequence of events
\{\mathcal{A}_{k}\}_{k=0}^{\infty}
. Let
\tau_{k_{0}}=\inf\{k\geq k_{0}:\mathcal{A}_{k}\text{ happens}\}
be the first index
k
after
k_{0}
such that
\mathcal{A}_{k}
happens. Suppose that for each realization of the sequence, there exists a (potentially random)
\widetilde{k}_{0}<\infty
such that
\tau_{\widetilde{k}_{0}}=\infty
(in other words,
\mathcal{A}_{k}
will finally not happen almost surely).
Also, for the sequence
\alpha_{k}=\iota_{1}/(k+1)^{p_{1}}
with
p_{1}\in(0,1]
, suppose there exists a deterministic
\bar{k}_{0}>0
such that for any fixed
k_{0}\geq\bar{k}_{0}
,
X_{k}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
. Then, for any constant
\zeta>0
satisfying
\zeta\iota_{1}>0.5
when
p_{1}=1
, we have

\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}).

We first decompose the primal-dual error term of Algorithm
1
. We have

\displaystyle{\bm{z}}_{k+1}={\bm{z}}_{k}-{\bar{\alpha}}_{k}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}={\bm{z}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-({\bar{\alpha}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}){\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}

\displaystyle=(1-\zeta\alpha_{k}){\bm{z}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}(\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}-{\widetilde{W}}_{k}{\bm{z}}_{k})-\zeta\alpha_{k}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k})-({\bar{\alpha}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}){\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}

\displaystyle=(1-\zeta\alpha_{k}){\bm{z}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}(\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}-W^{\star}{\bm{z}}_{k})-\zeta\alpha_{k}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}(W^{\star}-{\widetilde{W}}_{k}){\bm{z}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}(W^{\star})^{-1}({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k})

\displaystyle\quad-\zeta\alpha_{k}({\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}-(W^{\star})^{-1})({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k})-({\bar{\alpha}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}){\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}

\displaystyle=\prod_{i=0}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{i}){\bm{z}}_{0}-\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\left\{{\widetilde{W}}_{i}^{-1}(\nabla\mathcal{L}_{i}-W^{\star}{\bm{z}}_{i})+{\widetilde{W}}_{i}^{-1}(W^{\star}-{\widetilde{W}}_{i}){\bm{z}}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\left\{({\widetilde{W}}_{i}^{-1}-(W^{\star})^{-1})({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{i}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{i})+\frac{{\bar{\alpha}}_{i}-\zeta\alpha_{i}}{\zeta\alpha_{i}}{\widetilde{W}}_{i}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}(W^{\star})^{-1}({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{i}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{i})\eqqcolon{\mathcal{C}}_{1}^{k}-{\mathcal{C}}_{2}^{k}-{\mathcal{C}}_{3}^{k}-{\mathcal{C}}_{4}^{k}.

In the following proof, we choose the (deterministic) constant
\epsilon
to be sufficiently small such that, for each run of the algorithm, there exists a (potentially random)
\widetilde{k}_{0}<\infty
satisfying
\tau_{\widetilde{k}_{0}}(\epsilon)=\infty
, where
\tau_{k_{0}}(\epsilon)
is defined in (
4
).
This
\epsilon
exists because, for each run of the algorithm:

(a)

\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|>\epsilon^{2}
and
\|({\bm{x}}_{k},{\bm{\lambda}}_{k})\|>1/\epsilon
will finally not happen since (
32
) implies (
28
), and Lemma
4.4
shows that
{\bm{z}}_{k}\rightarrow 0
as
k\rightarrow\infty
almost surely.

(b)

\|\widetilde{W}_{k}^{-1}\|>1/\epsilon
,
\delta_{k}^{G}\neq{\bm{0}}
, and
\delta_{k}^{B}\neq{\bm{0}}
will finally not happen since (
32
) implies (
24
) and (
29
), and Lemmas
3.6
and
4.5
show that
\widetilde{W}_{k}\rightarrow W^{\star}=\nabla^{2}\mathcal{L}^{\star}
as
k\rightarrow\infty
almost surely.

(c)

\|\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}-\widetilde{W}_{k}{\bm{z}}_{k}\|>0.25\epsilon^{2}\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|
,
\|\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|>\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|/\epsilon
, and
\|\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}-W^{\star}{\bm{z}}_{k}\|>\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|^{2}/\epsilon
will
finally
not happen since
\nabla^{2}\mathcal{L}
is Lipschitz continuous near
({{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})
by Assumption
3.1
and
\widetilde{W}_{k}\rightarrow W^{\star}
.

(d)

\nu_{k}/(\tau_{k}\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c})\neq\zeta
will finally not happen by Assumption
4.3
.

\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{1}^{k}
, we follow (
C.4
), apply
\zeta\iota_{1}>0.5
when
p_{1}=1
, and have
{\mathcal{C}}_{1}^{k}=o(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{2}^{k}
, we apply Lemmas
4.6
and
4.7
, and have for
k\geq k_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}(\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}-W^{\star}{\bm{z}}_{k})

\displaystyle+{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}(W^{\star}-{\widetilde{W}}_{k}){\bm{z}}_{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{equ:def:tau}}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}+\frac{1}{\epsilon}\|{\widetilde{W}}_{k}-W^{\star}\|\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4}\right)+O_{p}\left(\left\{\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}/b_{k}\right\}\left\{\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}^{2}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}b_{k}+b_{k}^{3}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}/b_{k}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}b_{k}\right).

We note that

O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}b_{k}+b_{k}^{3}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}/b_{k}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}b_{k}\right)=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})\\
\Longleftarrow\min\{p_{2},0.5p_{2}+p_{3},3p_{3},0.5p_{2}+2p_{4}-p_{3},2p_{4}+p_{3}\}>0.5p_{1}\Longleftarrow\eqref{cond:4.8}.

Thus, we apply Lemma
C.3
and have
{\mathcal{C}}_{2}^{k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{3}^{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|({\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}-(W^{\star})^{-1})({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k})+\frac{{\bar{\alpha}}_{k}-\zeta\alpha_{k}}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}\right\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{equ:def:tau}}}}{{\leq}}\|{\widetilde{W}}_{k}^{-1}-(W^{\star})^{-1}\|\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}+\frac{\psi\alpha_{k}^{p-1}}{\epsilon\zeta}\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{equ:def:tau}}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\|(W^{\star})^{-1}\|}{\epsilon}\|{\widetilde{W}}_{k}-W^{\star}\|\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}+\frac{\psi\alpha_{k}^{p-1}}{\epsilon\zeta}\left\{\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k}\|+\frac{\|{\bm{z}}_{k}\|}{\epsilon}\right\}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\left\{\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}/b_{k}\right\}\left\{\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}^{2}\right\}\right)+O_{p}\left(\alpha_{k}^{p-1}\left(\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}b_{k}+b_{k}^{3}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}/b_{k}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}b_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{p-1}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\alpha_{k}^{p-1}b_{k}^{2}\right).

We note that

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\beta_{k}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}b_{k}+b_{k}^{3}+\sqrt{\beta_{k}}\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}/b_{k}+\widetilde{b}_{k}^{2}b_{k}+\alpha_{k}^{p-1}\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+\alpha_{k}^{p-1}b_{k}^{2}\right)=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})

\displaystyle\Longleftarrow\min\{p_{2},0.5p_{2}+p_{3},3p_{3},0.5p_{2}+2p_{4}-p_{3},2p_{4}+p_{3},(p-1)p_{1}+0.5p_{2},(p-1)p_{1}+2p_{3}\}>0.5p_{1}

\displaystyle\Longleftarrow\eqref{cond:4.8}.

Thus, we apply Lemma
C.3
again and have
{\mathcal{C}}_{3}^{k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{4}^{k}
, let us define
{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}},{\bm{\lambda}};\xi)\coloneqq{\widehat{\nabla}}F({\bm{x}};\xi)+{\widehat{\nabla}}c({\bm{x}})^{T}{\bm{\lambda}}
. We have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{4}^{k}=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}(W^{\star})^{-1}({\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{i}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{i})=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}{\bar{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}_{i}-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}_{i}\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{nequ:4}}}}{{=}}\;\;\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h-1},{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}_{i};\xi_{h})-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h-1},{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}({\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}])^{T}({\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}(\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-G_{h})^{T}({\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\cdot\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\eqqcolon{\mathcal{C}}_{4,1}^{k}+{\mathcal{C}}_{4,2}^{k}+{\mathcal{C}}_{4,3}^{k}+{\mathcal{C}}_{4,4}^{k}+{\mathcal{C}}_{4,5}^{k}.

\bullet
\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,1}^{k}
, we know from Lemma
C.3
that it suffices to show

\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h-1},{\bm{\lambda}}_{k})-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}_{k})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\cdot\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}).

Since
\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{k}\|\leq 1/\epsilon
when
\tau_{k_{0}}>k
, we apply Lemma
C.3
again and know that the above display is implied by

(\|\nabla f_{k-1}-\nabla f_{k}\|+\|G_{k-1}-G_{k}\|)\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}=o_{p}(\beta_{k}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}).

By the Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla f
and
G
, we know for
k\geq k_{0}+1
that

\displaystyle(\|\nabla f_{k-1}-\nabla f_{k}\|+\|G_{k-1}-G_{k}\|)\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}\leq(\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c})\|{\bm{x}}_{k-1}-{\bm{x}}_{k}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k-1}

\displaystyle\leq(\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c}){\bar{\alpha}}_{k-1}\|{\widetilde{\Delta}}{\bm{x}}_{k-1}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k-1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{equ:def:tau}}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{\epsilon}(\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c})(\zeta\alpha_{k-1}+\psi\alpha_{k-1}^{p})\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k-1}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon}(\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c})(\zeta\alpha_{k-1}+\psi\alpha_{k-1}^{p})(\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k-1}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k-1}\|+\|\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k-1}\|)\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k-1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\eqref{equ:def:tau}}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{\epsilon}(\kappa_{\nabla f}+\kappa_{\nabla c})(\zeta\alpha_{k-1}+\psi\alpha_{k-1}^{p})\left(\|{\bar{\nabla}}\mathcal{L}_{k-1}-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{k-1}\|+\frac{\|{\bm{z}}_{k-1}\|}{\epsilon}\right)\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k-1}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\alpha_{k}\left(\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}^{2}\right)\right),

where the last equality is due to Lemma
4.6
. We note that

O_{p}\left(\alpha_{k}\left(\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}^{2}\right)\right)=o_{p}(\beta_{k}\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})\Longleftarrow\min\{p_{1}+0.5p_{2},p_{1}+2p_{3}\}>p_{2}+0.5p_{1}\Longleftarrow\eqref{cond:4.8}.

Thus, we obtain
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,1}^{k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,2}^{k}
, we still apply Lemma
C.3
. We have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}({\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}])^{T}({\bm{\lambda}}_{k}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}+\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{k}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\|^{2}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\beta_{k}+b_{k}^{4})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{cond:4.8}}}{{=}}o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}),

where the second equality from the last is due to (
C.4
) and Lemma
4.6
. Thus, Lemma
C.3
suggests that
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,2}^{k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,3}^{k}
, we have a similar derivation. In particular, we note that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}(\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-G_{h})^{T}({\bm{\lambda}}_{k}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}c_{h}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-G_{h}\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{k}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(b_{k}^{2}(\sqrt{\beta_{k}}+b_{k}^{2}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{cond:4.8}}}{{=}}o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}),

where the second equality from the last is due to Lemmas
3.5
and
4.6
and (
C.11
). Thus, Lemma
C.3
suggests that
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,3}^{k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,4}^{k}
, we apply Lemma
3.5
and have

\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{k}\prod_{l=h+1}^{k}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]-\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}>k}=O(b_{k}^{2})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{cond:4.8}}}{{=}}o(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}).

Thus, Lemma
C.3
suggests that
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,4}^{k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
.

\bullet
\bullet
For
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,5}^{k}
, we aim to show

1/\sqrt{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\cdot{\mathcal{C}}_{4,5}^{k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathcal{N}({\bm{0}},\omega\cdot(W^{\star})^{-1}{\Omega^{\star}}(W^{\star})^{-1}).

(C.22)

We have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{4,5}^{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{k}\sum_{i=h}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}(W^{\star})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\\
{\bm{0}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{h}.

(C.23)

We claim that
\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\rightarrow(W^{\star})^{-1}{\Omega^{\star}}(W^{\star})^{-1}
as
h\rightarrow\infty
almost surely. In fact, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[({\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}])({\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}])^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]-\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]^{T}.

Since
\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\rightarrow\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}^{\star}={\bm{0}}
as
h\rightarrow\infty
by Lemma
3.5
, we only consider the first term. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[({\widehat{\nabla}}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})+{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})({\widehat{\nabla}}F({\bm{x}}_{h};\xi_{h})+{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4b_{h}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}_{h}^{-1}\left\{\delta\left(F({\bm{x}}_{h}\pm b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})+c^{T}({\bm{x}}_{h}\pm b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\right)\right\}^{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h}^{-T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4b_{h}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}_{h}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}_{h}^{T}\int_{-b_{h}}^{b_{h}}\int_{-b_{h}}^{b_{h}}\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})ds_{1}ds_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h}^{-T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right],

(C.24)

where in the second equality, we follow the definition in (
10
) and define

\delta\left(F({\bm{x}}_{h}\pm b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})+c^{T}({\bm{x}}_{h}\pm b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\right)\coloneqq\left(F({\bm{x}}_{h}+b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})+c^{T}({\bm{x}}_{h}+b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\right)\\
-\left(F({\bm{x}}_{h}-b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})+c^{T}({\bm{x}}_{h}-b_{h}\bm{\Delta}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\right).

For (
C.7
), we first condition on both
{\bm{x}}_{h}
and
\bm{\Delta}_{h}
, and focus on the integrand.
For each run of the algorithm, we consider
h
to be sufficiently large (with a potentially random threshold index) such that
{\bm{x}}_{h}\in\{{\bm{x}}:\|{\bm{x}}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}\|\leq\delta^{\prime}\}
, where
\delta^{\prime}\in(0,\delta)
is chosen to ensure that
{\bm{x}}+s\bm{\Delta}\in\{{\bm{x}}:\|{\bm{x}}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}\|\leq\delta\}
for any
s\in[-b_{h},b_{h}]
and
\bm{\Delta}\sim{\mathcal{P}}_{\bm{\Delta}}
. For the above
{\bm{x}}_{h}
and any
-b_{h}\leq s_{1},s_{2}\leq b_{h}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi)\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})\nabla^{T}F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\nabla^{T}F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\nabla f({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h})({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}G({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h})-\nabla f^{\star}({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}G^{\star}

\displaystyle\quad+G^{T}({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\nabla^{T}f({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h})-(G^{\star})^{T}{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\nabla^{T}f^{\star}

\displaystyle\quad+G^{T}({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h}){\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}G({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h})-(G^{\star})^{T}{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}({\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}G^{\star}.

(C.25)

For the first term in (
C.7
), we can further bound it as

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})\nabla^{T}F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\nabla^{T}F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|\cdot\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|\cdot\|\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|\cdot\|\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{q=1}^{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|^{2}\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|^{2}]\right\}^{1/2}\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\|^{2}\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]\right\}^{1/2}.

Note from Assumptions
4.2
and
3.3
that for
q=1,2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})-\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi_{h})\right\|^{2}\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\nabla^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h}+t({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}});\xi_{h})({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}})dt\right\|^{2}\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla^{2}F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h}+t({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}});\xi_{h})\|^{2}\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}]dt\cdot\|{\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{q}\bm{\Delta}_{h}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O(\|{\bm{x}}_{h}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}}\|^{2}+b_{h}^{2})\rightarrow 0\quad\text{ as }\;\;h\rightarrow\infty.

The above two displays imply almost surely,

\max_{-b_{h}\leq s_{1},s_{2}\leq b_{h}}\big\{\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{1}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})\nabla^{T}F({\bm{x}}_{h}+s_{2}\bm{\Delta}_{h};\xi_{h})\mid{\bm{x}}_{h},\bm{\Delta}_{h}\right]\\
-\mathbb{E}[\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)\nabla^{T}F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)]\big\}\rightarrow 0\quad\text{ as }\;\;h\rightarrow\infty.

For the second, third, and fourth terms in (
C.7
), it is trivial to verify that they achieve the same almost sure convergence as the above display, due to the Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla f
and
G
and the fact that
|s_{1}|,|s_{2}|\leq b_{h}\rightarrow\infty
. Therefore, we combine (
C.7
) and (
C.7
) and obtain almost surely,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{h},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{h})\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\longrightarrow\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi)\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi)\right]\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}\text{Cov}\left(\nabla_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi)\right)\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}\text{Cov}\left(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)\right)\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}\right].

(C.26)

This, together with (
C.7
) and the definition of
\bm{\phi}_{h}
in (
C.7
), implies
\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]\rightarrow(W^{\star})^{-1}{\Omega^{\star}}(W^{\star})^{-1}
as
h\rightarrow\infty
almost surely. With this result, we then analyze the conditional variance process. We have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{h=0}^{k}\sum_{i=h}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}=h}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})\beta_{h}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{h=0}^{\min\{i,i^{\prime}\}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})\beta_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{h=0}^{i^{\prime}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})\beta_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{2}\zeta^{2}\alpha_{i}^{2}\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})^{2}\beta_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{2}\zeta\alpha_{i}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{i}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})(1-\beta_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{h=0}^{i^{\prime}}\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})^{2}\beta_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{2}\zeta^{2}\alpha_{i}^{2}\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})^{2}\beta_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}].

We apply Lemma
A.2
and note that

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{i\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\beta_{i}}\sum_{h=0}^{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})^{2}\beta_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]=\frac{1}{2}(W^{\star})^{-1}{\Omega^{\star}}(W^{\star})^{-1},

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{i\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{i}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{i}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})(1-\beta_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\beta_{i^{\prime}}=1,

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{k\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{2}\zeta^{2}\alpha_{i}^{2}=\omega\coloneqq\begin{cases}0.5,&\text{if }\;p_{1}\in(0,1),\\
\frac{\zeta\iota_{1}}{2\zeta\iota_{1}-1},&\text{if }\;p_{1}=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{k\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{2}\zeta^{2}\alpha_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}=0.

Combining the above two displays, we obtain almost surely,

\lim\limits_{k\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\phi}_{h}\bm{\phi}_{h}^{T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]=\omega\cdot(W^{\star})^{-1}{\Omega^{\star}}(W^{\star})^{-1}.

(C.27)

Next, we verify the Lindeberg condition. We aim to show that for any
\epsilon>0
,

\lim\limits_{k\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\alpha_{k}}\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\bm{\phi}_{h}\|^{2}1_{\|a_{h,k}\bm{\phi}_{h}\|\geq\epsilon\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}\right]\leq\lim\limits_{k\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\epsilon\alpha_{k}^{1.5}}\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{3}\mathbb{E}[\|\bm{\phi}_{h}\|^{3}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]=0.

(C.28)

Since
r\geq 3
in (
32
), we know from (
B.2
) that
\mathbb{E}[\|\bm{\phi}_{h}\|^{3}\mid\mathcal{F}_{h-1}]
is uniformly bounded. Thus, it suffices to show
\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{3}=o(\alpha_{k}^{1.5})
. We have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{k}\sum_{i=h}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}=h}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime\prime}=h}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\beta_{h}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})\beta_{h}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\quad\prod_{j^{\prime\prime}=i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime\prime}}\prod_{l^{\prime\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime\prime}})\beta_{h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime\prime}=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\prod_{j^{\prime\prime}=i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime\prime}}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\quad\sum_{h=0}^{\min\{i,i^{\prime},i^{\prime\prime}\}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})\prod_{l^{\prime\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime\prime}})\beta_{h}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq 6\sum_{i=0}^{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\sum_{i^{\prime\prime}=0}^{i^{\prime}}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\zeta\alpha_{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\prod_{j^{\prime\prime}=i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime\prime}}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\sum_{h=0}^{i^{\prime\prime}}\prod_{l=h+1}^{i}(1-\beta_{l})\prod_{l^{\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime}})\prod_{l^{\prime\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime\prime}})\beta_{h}^{3}\quad\quad(i\geq i^{\prime}\geq i^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle=6\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{3}\zeta\alpha_{i}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{i}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})^{2}(1-\beta_{j^{\prime}})\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\sum_{i^{\prime\prime}=0}^{i^{\prime}}\prod_{j^{\prime\prime}=i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime\prime}})(1-\beta_{j^{\prime\prime}})^{2}\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{h=0}^{i^{\prime\prime}}\prod_{l^{\prime\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime\prime}})^{3}\beta_{h}^{3}.

We apply Lemma
A.2
and
(
Na and Mahoney 2025
, Lemma B.3(b))
and note that

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{i^{\prime\prime}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\beta_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{2}}\sum_{h=0}^{i^{\prime\prime}}\prod_{l^{\prime\prime}=h+1}^{i^{\prime\prime}}(1-\beta_{l^{\prime\prime}})^{3}\beta_{h}^{3}=\frac{1}{3},

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{i^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}}\beta_{i^{\prime}}}\sum_{i^{\prime\prime}=0}^{i^{\prime}}\prod_{j^{\prime\prime}=i^{\prime\prime}+1}^{i^{\prime}}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime\prime}})(1-\beta_{j^{\prime\prime}})^{2}\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime\prime}}\beta_{i^{\prime\prime}}^{2}=\frac{1}{2},

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{i\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{(\zeta\alpha_{i})^{2}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\prod_{j^{\prime}=i^{\prime}+1}^{i}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j^{\prime}})^{2}(1-\beta_{j^{\prime}})(\zeta\alpha_{i^{\prime}})^{2}\beta_{i^{\prime}}=1,

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{k\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{(\zeta\alpha_{k})^{1.5}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})^{3}(\zeta\alpha_{i})^{3}=0,

where the last equality applies
\zeta\iota_{1}>0.5
when
p_{1}=1
. Thus, we have
\sum_{h=0}^{k}a_{h,k}^{3}=o(\alpha_{k}^{1.5})
. By the central limit theorem of martingale arrays
(
Hall and Heyde 2014
, Corollary 3.1)
, the results (
C.27
) and (
C.28
) lead to (
C.22
).

Finally, we combine the result of
{\mathcal{C}}_{4,5}^{k}
in (
C.22
) with all the results of
{\mathcal{C}}_{1}^{k},{\mathcal{C}}_{2}^{k},{\mathcal{C}}_{3}^{k},{\mathcal{C}}_{4,1}^{k},{\mathcal{C}}_{4,2}^{k},{\mathcal{C}}_{4,3}^{k},{\mathcal{C}}_{4,4}^{k}
, for which we have shown that each is of order
o_{p}(\sqrt{\alpha_{k}})
. We obtain

1/\sqrt{\zeta\alpha_{k}}\cdot({\bm{x}}_{k}-{{\bm{x}}^{\star}},{\bm{\lambda}}_{k}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathcal{N}({\bm{0}},\omega\cdot(W^{\star})^{-1}{\Omega^{\star}}(W^{\star})^{-1}).

Noting that
{\bar{\alpha}}_{k}/(\zeta\alpha_{k})\rightarrow 1
almost surely and applying Slutsky’s theorem, we complete the proof.

C.8
Proof of Lemma
C.3

We aim to show that for any
\epsilon,\delta>0
, there exists
K=K(\epsilon,\delta)>0
such that for any
k\geq K(\epsilon,\delta)
,

P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|\geq\epsilon\right)\leq\delta.

(C.29)

For the above fixed
\epsilon,\delta>0
, we know from
P(\cup_{k_{0}=0}^{\infty}\{\tau_{k_{0}}=\infty\})=1
that

\displaystyle P\left(\bigcap_{k_{0}=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{B}_{k_{0}}\right)\coloneqq P\left(\bigcap_{k_{0}=0}^{\infty}\bigcup_{k_{0}^{\prime}\geq k_{0}}\bigcup_{k\geq k_{0}^{\prime}}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}^{\prime}}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}\leq i}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right\}\right)=0.

Since

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{k_{0}+1}

\displaystyle=\bigcup_{k_{0}^{\prime}\geq k_{0}+1}\bigcup_{k\geq k_{0}^{\prime}}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}^{\prime}}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}+1}\leq i}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right\}

\displaystyle\subseteq\bigcup_{k_{0}^{\prime}\geq k_{0}+1}\bigcup_{k\geq k_{0}^{\prime}}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}^{\prime}}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}\leq i}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right\}\quad(\text{ since }\tau_{k_{0}+1}\geq\tau_{k_{0}})

\displaystyle\subseteq\bigcup_{k_{0}^{\prime}\geq k_{0}}\bigcup_{k\geq k_{0}^{\prime}}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}^{\prime}}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}}\leq i}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right\}=\mathcal{B}_{k_{0}},

the above two displays imply that
\lim_{k_{0}\rightarrow\infty}P(\mathcal{B}_{k_{0}})=0
. Thus, there exists
k_{0}(\delta)\geq\bar{k}_{0}
such that for any
k\geq k_{0}(\delta)
,

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}\leq i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}\leq i}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\bigcup_{k\geq k_{0}(\delta)}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}|\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}\leq i}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right\}\right)\leq P(\mathcal{B}_{k_{0}(\delta)})\leq\frac{\delta}{3}.

(C.30)

For the above
k_{0}(\delta)
fixed, we apply Lemma
A.2
and have

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}>i}=o_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}^{1.5}\right)=o_{p}(1).

Thus, there exists
K^{1}=K^{1}(\epsilon,\delta)\geq k_{0}(\delta)
such that for any
k\geq K^{1}(\epsilon,\delta)
,

P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}>i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)\leq\frac{\delta}{3}.

(C.31)

Finally, we note that with probability 1,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=0}^{k_{0}(\delta)-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\sum_{i=0}^{k_{0}(\delta)-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k_{0}(\delta)-1}\left|(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|\cdot\prod_{j=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\left|1-\zeta\alpha_{j}\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{nequ:7}}}{{\longrightarrow}}0\quad\;\;\text{ as }\;\;k\rightarrow\infty.

This implies that

P\left(\bigcap_{k\geq k_{0}(\delta)}\mathcal{C}_{k}\right)\coloneqq P\left(\bigcap_{k\geq k_{0}(\delta)}\bigcup_{k^{\prime}\geq k}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=0}^{k_{0}(\delta)-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k^{\prime}}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right\}\right)=0.

Since
\mathcal{C}_{k+1}\subseteq\mathcal{C}_{k}
, we have
\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}P(\mathcal{C}_{k})=0
. Thus, there exists
K^{2}(\epsilon,\delta)\geq k_{0}(\delta)
such that for any
k\geq K^{2}(\epsilon,\delta)
,

P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=0}^{k_{0}(\delta)-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)\leq P(\mathcal{C}_{k})\leq\frac{\delta}{3}.

(C.32)

Combining (
C.8
), (
C.31
), (
C.32
), and letting
K(\epsilon,\delta)\coloneqq\max\{K^{1}(\epsilon,\delta),K^{2}(\epsilon,\delta)\}
, we have
\forall k\geq K(\epsilon,\delta)
,

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=0}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|\geq\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=0}^{k_{0}(\delta)-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)+P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}>i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{k}}}\left|\sum_{i=k_{0}(\delta)}^{k}\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}(1-\zeta\alpha_{j})\alpha_{i}X_{i}\bm{1}_{\tau_{k_{0}(\delta)}\leq i}\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{3}\right)\leq\frac{\delta}{3}+\frac{\delta}{3}+\frac{\delta}{3}=\delta.

This verifies (
C.29
) and completes the proof.

C.9
Proof of Proposition
4.9

By the definition of
\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}
,
\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{op}
and
{\Omega^{\star}}
, we note that

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}-\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{op}

\displaystyle=(W^{\star})^{-1}\left({\Omega^{\star}}-{\rm diag}\left(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)),{\bm{0}}\right)\right)(W^{\star})^{-1}

\displaystyle=(W^{\star})^{-1}{\rm diag}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi))\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}\right]-\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)),\;{\bm{0}}\right)(W^{\star})^{-1}

\displaystyle=(W^{\star})^{-1}{\rm diag}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi))\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right],\;{\bm{0}}\right)(W^{\star})^{-1}\succeq{\bm{0}},

where the third equality is due to
\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}]=\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}]=I
by Assumption
3.3
. For the second part of the result, we follow the above result and have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}-\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{op}\|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\|W^{\star}\|^{2}}\left\|{\rm diag}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi))\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right],\;{\bm{0}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\|W^{\star}\|^{2}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi))\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)))}{\|W^{\star}\|^{2}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)))}{\|W^{\star}\|^{2}}\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Delta}^{T}\bm{\Delta}\cdot\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}]-I\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)))}{\|W^{\star}\|^{2}}\cdot(d-1)\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Delta}^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{\bm{\Delta}^{2}}]\quad(\text{by Assumption \ref{ass:Delta}}).

On the other hand, we also have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}-\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{op}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(W^{\star})^{-1}\|^{2}\left\|{\rm diag}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi))\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right],\;{\bm{0}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=\|(W^{\star})^{-1}\|^{2}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi))\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(W^{\star})^{-1}\|^{2}\lambda_{\max}(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)))\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}-I\right)\left(\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}-I\right)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=\|(W^{\star})^{-1}\|^{2}\lambda_{\max}(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)))\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Delta}^{T}\bm{\Delta}\cdot\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}]-I\right\|

\displaystyle=\|(W^{\star})^{-1}\|^{2}\lambda_{\max}(\text{Cov}(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)))\cdot(d-1)\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Delta}^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{\bm{\Delta}^{2}}].

This completes the proof.

C.10
Proof of Proposition
4.10

By Lemmas
3.6
and
4.5
, we know
{\widetilde{W}}_{k}\rightarrow W^{\star}
as
k\rightarrow\infty
almost surely. Thus, it suffices to show

\frac{1}{k+1}\sum_{t=0}^{k}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}F({\bm{x}}_{t};\xi_{t})+{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{t}){\bm{\lambda}}_{t}\right)\left({\widehat{\nabla}}F({\bm{x}}_{t};\xi_{t})+{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{t}){\bm{\lambda}}_{t}\right)^{T}\\
\longrightarrow\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}\text{Cov}\left(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)\right)\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}\right]\quad\text{ as }\;\;k\rightarrow\infty\;\;\text{ almost surely}.

Recall from the proof of
{\mathcal{C}}_{4}^{k}
in Appendix
C.7
that we define
{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t})\coloneqq{\widehat{\nabla}}F({\bm{x}}_{t};\xi_{t})+{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{t}){\bm{\lambda}}_{t}
.
Since
r\geq 4
, we apply (
B.2
) and the strong law of large number for square integrable martingales
(
Duflo 1997
, Theorem 1.3.15)
, and know that

\frac{1}{k+1}\sum_{t=0}^{k}\left({\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right)\rightarrow 0

(C.33)

as
k\rightarrow\infty
almost surely. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{t})({\bm{\lambda}}_{t}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}){\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}f({\bm{x}}_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]+\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}f({\bm{x}}_{t})({\bm{\lambda}}_{t}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}{\widehat{\nabla}}c({\bm{x}}_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}[{\widehat{\nabla}}^{T}c({\bm{x}}_{t})({\bm{\lambda}}_{t}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})({\bm{\lambda}}_{t}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star})^{T}{\widehat{\nabla}}c({\bm{x}}_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle=O(\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{t}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\|+\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{t}-{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star}\|^{2})\rightarrow 0\quad\text{ as }\;\;t\rightarrow\infty\;\;\text{ almost surely},

where the second equality is due to the boundedness of
{\widehat{\nabla}}f({\bm{x}}_{t})
and
{\widehat{\nabla}}c({\bm{x}}_{t})
, which is as shown in (
B.2
). Therefore, the Stolz–Cesar̀o theorem suggests that

\frac{1}{k+1}\sum_{t=0}^{k}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}_{t};\xi_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right)\rightarrow 0

as
k\rightarrow\infty
almost surely. Finally, applying (
C.7
) and the Stolz–Cesar̀o theorem again, we obtain

\frac{1}{k+1}\sum_{t=0}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{t}){\widehat{\nabla}}_{{\bm{x}}}^{T}\mathcal{L}({\bm{x}}_{t},{\bm{\lambda}}^{\star};\xi_{t})\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\rightarrow\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Delta}^{T}\text{Cov}\left(\nabla F({{\bm{x}}^{\star}};\xi)\right)\bm{\Delta}\bm{\Delta}^{-T}\right]

as
k\rightarrow\infty
almost surely. Combining the above two displays with (
C.33
), we complete the proof.
